# Agents · G · part 7

990 entries, github-actions-security to glue-incremental-performance-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **github-actions-security**: Rules for every file under `.github/workflows`, and the audit of the current workflows against them. Version pinning policy for dependencies lives in [Dependency updates](dependency-updates.md). Each privileged workflow's design lives in its ADR; this page says what any workflow may do. Where step code lives and how a job loads it is in [GitHub Actions workflows](github-actions.md). ([martin-francois/symphony-trello](https://github.com/martin-francois/symphony-trello/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/github-actions-security.md) · ★5)

- **github-orchestrator:conflict-resolver**: Use this agent to predict which open pull requests will conflict before they do, and to resolve existing conflicts by reconstructing a result that satisfies both sides rather than picking ours or theirs. - **github-orchestrator:correctness-reviewer**: Use this agent as the correctness lens of the review board. - **github-orchestrator:dedup-detective**: Use this agent to detect duplicate issues semantically — matching on symptom, stack trace, and affected surface rather than title wording — and to search closed issues, where a previously-closed duplicate is itself a signal. - **github-orchestrator:dependency-steward**: Use this agent to report dependency health and drive upgrades — batching by risk so each pull request has a single failure hypothesis, keeping the lockfile in sync, and flagging unmaintained or suspicious packages. - **github-orchestrator:docs-reviewer**: Use this agent as the documentation lens of the review board. - **github-orchestrator:dora-analyst**: Use this agent to compute deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, and time to restore from real repository history, decomposed so that every regression points to a specific stage and cause. - **github-orchestrator:ownership-mapper**: Use this agent to find ownership drift, uncovered and orphaned CODEOWNERS paths, and bus-factor-one modules, and to route reviewers on a pull request by ownership, recent authorship, and current load. - **github-orchestrator:performance-reviewer**: Use this agent as the performance lens of the review board. - **global-auditor**: 专利稿件 md 草稿的全局规则与短块审计员(多路审查之一)。权要一稿 / 全文一稿(含直写全文稿的权要闸门与全文闸门)的 md 草稿写完、进入 DOCX 前,以及权要返修/全文返修的增量复核轮,均与本阶段其他专审 auditor 并行调用。负责完整性一票否决、global.md 全局项(G3-G6)、全文术语一致性和短块规则;不写文件、不改 DOCX、不越权读取未传入的规则。 ([q1480371991/patent-harness](https://github.com/q1480371991/patent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/global-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **global-checker**: 多言語での意味・発音・国際展開の専門家。 brand-strategist が提案するアプリ名候補の国際展開可能性を評価する。 app-naming チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/marketing/app-naming/agents/global-checker.md) · ★38 · 3 copies)
- **global-CLAUDE**: Write working files (notes, handoff briefs, screenshots, probe scripts, test logs, HTML reports) into the Obsidian vault at `~/notes/scratchpads/&lt;project&gt;/`, in a task-named subfolder. The session scratchpad and `/tmp` hold only throwaway intermediates. ([mwamodo/bin](https://github.com/mwamodo/bin/blob/HEAD/agents/global-CLAUDE.md) · ★1)
- **global-claude**: I am Ali, a seasoned software engineer and an engineering manager. Skip explanations of standard engineering concepts unless I ask for them. ([aminfara/dotfiles](https://github.com/aminfara/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/global-claude.md))
- **global-code-reviewer**: Reviews changed code and tests for standards conformance and plan-scope completeness. Use for diff review, standards review, and implementation gate checks. Report only real rule violations; no style preferences or speculation. ([Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli](https://github.com/Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli/blob/HEAD/augur-cli/.github/agents/0-global-code-reviewer.agent.md))
- **global-contract**: - Understand the user's intended outcome and identify the constraints that actually govern it. Ground constraints in the user's request, explicit commitments, and observed dependencies. Treat existing artifacts, configuration, and earlier proposals as evidence to examine; their existence alone does not make them requirements. - Interpret requests by substance, not grammar. Requests to explain, rev ([wangl-cc/dotfiles](https://github.com/wangl-cc/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/.chezmoitemplates/agents/global-contract.md) · ★8)
- **global-customization-author**: Authors and updates .github customization artifacts: agent specs, skills, prompts, and instructions. Use when adding or updating any .github/agents/*.agent.md, .github/skills/**, .github/prompts/*.prompt.md, or .github/instructions/** file. Scans adjacent and linked .md files for required companion updates before finishing, including keeping .github/copilot-instructions.md synchronized when routing or capabilities change. ([Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli](https://github.com/Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli/blob/HEAD/augur-cli/.github/agents/0-global-customization-author.agent.md))
- **global-customization-reviewer**: Reviews .github customization artifacts for standards conformance, dead links, and routing correctness. Use after customization-author delivers a new or updated agent spec, skill, prompt, or instruction. Read-only. Does not modify files. ([Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli](https://github.com/Kenneth-Posey/augur-cli/blob/HEAD/augur-cli/.github/agents/0-global-customization-reviewer.agent.md))

- **glossary**: - **可信回答**：由 Core 呈现的问题绑定到同宿主、呈现之后、唯一且未消费的用户事件；原生表单回答在同一次调用内以同一 interaction 落账。 - **执行授权依据**：决定是否允许进入实现的稳定语义摘要，包含 scope、执行相关任务/RU、文件范围、恢复语义和当前阻断风险，不包含 review batch id 或阶段投影。 - **证据失效**：已记录的验证、Trace、Review 或 checkpoint 不再满足其引用依据时的显式 stale 状态；失效不会被自动当作风险接受。 - **工作区归属**：路径是否属于当前 feature 的用户结论。已观察不等于已归属；未知路径必须通过批量或逐个 interaction 处理。 - **宿主接线健康**：Claude/Codex adapter 在项目级记录的 SessionStart 或 UserPromptSu ([wxy-hh/dev-flow](https://github.com/wxy-hh/dev-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/glossary.md) · ★2)

- **GLOSSARY**: - **hive** [fleet] — every session JIVA runs for one repo, on its private tmux server (`tmux -L jiva-&lt;repo&gt;`). - **keeper** [human] — you. Sits outside the hive, answers asks, runs the KEEPER-only lines. Never a session. - **cell** [seat] — one slot in the hive that holds a session: `desk`, `queen`, `w1`…`wN`. Bodies die; the cell stays. - **body / soul** — body: one Claude Code session. soul: the ([MeiuxMeiux/jiva](https://github.com/MeiuxMeiux/jiva/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/GLOSSARY.md))
- **glossary**: - **Atari XL/XE** — 8-bit home computer family (800XL, 130XE). 6502 CPU at ~1.79 MHz. Target hardware for the current Atari implementation. - **ANTIC** — display list processor. Reads display lists from RAM, drives raster fetch, generates display interrupts. Custom chip. - **GTIA** — graphics translator. Converts ANTIC pixel data + sprite data to NTSC/PAL video. Palette chip. - **POKEY** — pot key ([mikolajmikolajczyk/madside](https://github.com/mikolajmikolajczyk/madside/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/glossary.md))
